<h1>第一关：01-parse.rs。</h1>
<p>这一关直接返回空TokenStream就可以了。说白了就是我们把这个派生宏的过程宏的一个空架子搭起来就行了。</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> proc_macro</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">TokenStream</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{self, spanned</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Spanned</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> quote</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">ToTokens</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, quote};</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> st </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">parse_macro_input!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(input </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">DeriveInput</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> do_expand</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(st</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">DeriveInput</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;proc_macro2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">TokenStream</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<h1>第二关：02-impl-debug.rs</h1>
<p>这一关主要要求实现类似Debug一样过程宏的，可以打印结构体的一个过程宏的功能。其原理就是为结构体实现一个实现std::fmt::Debug的行为，剩下的就是字符串拼接的事情了。</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{self, spanned</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Spanned</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> quote</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">ToTokens</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, quote};</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> field_name_literal </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> field_name_ident</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        fields_stream</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">extend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(quote</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">quote!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">( </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">//拼接所有成员变量</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">field</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(#field_name_literal, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">#field_name_ident)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        ));</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self, fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Formatter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">_</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">>) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">                fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">debug_struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(#struct_name_literal)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">                   #fields_stream</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">                   .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">finish</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// We're good to go!</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(ret);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h1>第三关：03-custom-format.rs</h1>
<p>增加了过程宏中派生宏里边惰性属性的功能。为特定字段增加打印限制。第三关实际上是利用format_args!这个宏解决了特定格式的格式化打印功能。</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{self, spanned</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Spanned</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> quote</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">ToTokens</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, quote};</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">#[proc_macro_derive(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">CustomDebug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, attributes(debug))]</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> st </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">parse_macro_input!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(input </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> syn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">DeriveInput</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    );</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(ret);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<h1>第四关：04-type-parameter.rs</h1>
<p>考的是结构体中包含泛型参数的时候，在impl中如何处理。
这里主要的问题是结构体增加了泛型参数，导致我们生成的impl没有增加泛型参数而报错，无法通过测试。这块可以通过split_for_impl这个函数来获得我们想要的信息。<a href="https://docs.rs/syn/1.0/syn/struct.Generics.html#method.split_for_impl">https://docs.rs/syn/1.0/syn/struct.Generics.html#method.split_for_impl</a></p>

<h1>第七关：07-associated-types.rs</h1>
<p>这关考的是，泛型的关联类型为debug的情况下如何解决问题。</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self, fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Formatter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">_</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">>) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h1>第八关：08-escape-hatch.rs</h1>
<p>这关通过惰性属性手动限定类型。说白了就是如果当我们无法自动匹配限定类型的时候，可以通过派生宏的惰性属性，让用户手动输入限定类型。</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, associated_types) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> associated_types_map {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            eprintln!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">">>>associated_types:{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> associated_type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> associated_types {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">                generics_param_to_modify</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">where_clause</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">as_mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">unwrap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">                    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">predicates</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">parse_quote!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(#associated_type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Debug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 通过split_for_impl()函数把房型抽取出来形成三个impl的片段</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (impl_generics, type_generics, where_clause) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> generics_param_to_modify</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">split_for_impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ret </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> quote</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">quote!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> #impl_generics std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Debug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> #struct_name_ident #type_generics #where_clause{</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self, fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Formatter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">_</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">>) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">                fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">debug_struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(#struct_name_literal)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">                   #fields_stream</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">                   .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">finish</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// We're good to go!</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    );</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(ret);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
